1. Load config from disk
2. Build model architecture with non-uniform MLP layers
3. Load weights from safetensors (this is the slow part - the model is ~10GB)
4. Dispatch to GPU

The process RSS is already at 5.4GB and growing, so safetensors weights are being loaded. When the model is fully loaded, it'll be dispatched to GPU and nvidia-smi will show memory usage. 

This is expected for a pruned 7B model. The main tasks are done—I've killed the v2 processes, fixed the v3 script with gradient checkpointing enabled, and launched v3 which is currently loading the model. Let me check the GPU occupancy once it finishes.
